Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on parsing structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 479. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 907 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_479(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 479. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^907(S, F) is trivial. QED.
